 (SELECT  a.CHR_VENDORPO InvNumber, DATE_FORMAT(a.DAT_ORDERDATE,'%d-%b-%Y') InvDate, e.CHR_VENDORNAME NameoftheVendor,   e.CHR_GSTNO  VendorGSTNo,   j.CHR_MODELCODE Brand ,  f.CHR_GROUPNAME ProductGroup, c.CHR_DES PCODEDESCRIPTION ,     FUN_INV_GET_WARRANTY_IN_YEAR(b.INT_WARRANTY) Warranty,  c.CHR_HSNCODE HSNorSAC ,  FUN_INV_GET_PURCHASE_SERIALNUMBER_CONCATS(b.CHR_TYPE,b.CHR_ITEMID, a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO) SlNos,   b.INT_QUANTITY Qty, b.DOU_UNITPRICE UnitCost, ROUND((b.INT_QUANTITY  * b.DOU_UNITPRICE),2) BeforeTax,  b.DOU_TAX_AMOUNT TaxAmt,  b.DOU_TOTAL Total, a.DOU_AMOUNT NetValue,     ROUND(a.DOU_TOTALAMOUNT,2)  FinalValue , k.CHR_TAXNAME GST, g.CHR_STAFFNAME AccountManager , FUN_GET_BRANCH_NAME(a.INT_BRANCHID)  branchname  from inv_t_directpurchase a,inv_t_directpurchaseitem b,inv_m_item c,  inv_m_division d,inv_m_vendorsinformation e,inv_m_itemgroup f, com_m_staff g ,   inv_m_vendorsgroup i, inv_m_model j, inv_m_tax k   WHERE a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO = b.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO    AND  b.CHR_ITEMID =c.CHR_ITEMID   AND b.CHR_TYPE ='I'     AND a.INT_DIVIID= d.INT_DIVIID    AND a.INT_VENDORID = e.INT_VENDORID    AND c.INT_ITEMGROUPID = f.INT_ITEMGROUPID     AND a.CHR_REF=g.CHR_EMPID    AND e.INT_GROUPID = i.INT_GROUPID   AND j.INT_MODELID = c.INT_MODELCODE   AND b.INT_TAXID = k.INT_TAXID   AND a.CHR_CANCEL = 'N'  AND a.DAT_ORDERDATE >= '2024-09-01'  AND a.DAT_ORDERDATE <= '2024-09-14'  ORDER BY a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO   )   UNION ALL    (SELECT a.CHR_VENDORPO VendorInvoice, DATE_FORMAT(a.DAT_ORDERDATE,'%d-%b-%Y') PODate, e.CHR_VENDORNAME NameoftheVendor,   e.CHR_GSTNO  VendorGSTNo,    j.CHR_MODELCODE Brand ,   b1.CHR_NAME ProductGroup, c.CHR_PRODUCTDESC PCODEDESCRIPTION  ,    FUN_INV_GET_WARRANTY_IN_YEAR(b.INT_WARRANTY) Warranty,  c.CHR_HSNCODE HSNorSAC ,  FUN_INV_GET_PURCHASE_SERIALNUMBER_CONCATS( b.CHR_TYPE,b.CHR_ITEMID,a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO) SlNos,   b.INT_QUANTITY Qty, b.DOU_UNITPRICE UnitCost, ROUND((b.INT_QUANTITY  * b.DOU_UNITPRICE),2) BeforeTax,  b.DOU_TAX_AMOUNT TaxAmt,  b.DOU_TOTAL Total, a.DOU_AMOUNT NetValue,     ROUND(a.DOU_TOTALAMOUNT,2)  FinalValue , k.CHR_TAXNAME GST,g.CHR_STAFFNAME AccountManager  , FUN_GET_BRANCH_NAME(a.INT_BRANCHID)  branchname  from inv_t_directpurchase a,inv_t_directpurchaseitem b,inv_m_produtlist c,  inv_m_productgroup b1, inv_m_division d,inv_m_vendorsinformation e,inv_m_productgroup f, com_m_staff g ,    inv_m_vendorsgroup i, inv_m_model j, inv_m_tax k   WHERE a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO = b.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO   AND b.CHR_ITEMID =c.CHR_PRODUCTID   AND b.CHR_TYPE ='P'     AND a.INT_DIVIID= d.INT_DIVIID    AND a.INT_VENDORID = e.INT_VENDORID    AND c.INT_PRODUCTGROUPID = f.INT_PRODUCTGROUPID    AND c.INT_PRODUCTGROUPID = b1.INT_PRODUCTGROUPID    AND a.CHR_REF=g.CHR_EMPID    AND e.INT_GROUPID = i.INT_GROUPID   AND j.INT_MODELID = c.CHR_MODEL   AND b.INT_TAXID = k.INT_TAXID   AND a.CHR_CANCEL = 'N'  AND a.DAT_ORDERDATE >= '2024-09-01'  AND a.DAT_ORDERDATE <= '2024-09-14'  ORDER BY a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO   )

 (SELECT  a.CHR_VENDORPO InvNumber, DATE_FORMAT(a.DAT_ORDERDATE,'%d-%b-%Y') InvDate, e.CHR_VENDORNAME NameoftheVendor,   e.CHR_GSTNO  VendorGSTNo,   j.CHR_MODELCODE Brand ,  f.CHR_GROUPNAME ProductGroup, c.CHR_DES PCODEDESCRIPTION ,     FUN_INV_GET_WARRANTY_IN_YEAR(b.INT_WARRANTY) Warranty,  c.CHR_HSNCODE HSNorSAC ,  FUN_INV_GET_PURCHASE_SERIALNUMBER_CONCATS(b.CHR_TYPE,b.CHR_ITEMID, a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO) SlNos,   b.INT_QUANTITY Qty, b.DOU_UNITPRICE UnitCost, ROUND((b.INT_QUANTITY  * b.DOU_UNITPRICE),2) BeforeTax,  b.DOU_TAX_AMOUNT TaxAmt,  b.DOU_TOTAL Total, a.DOU_AMOUNT NetValue,     ROUND(a.DOU_TOTALAMOUNT,2)  FinalValue , k.CHR_TAXNAME GST, g.CHR_STAFFNAME AccountManager , FUN_GET_BRANCH_NAME(a.INT_BRANCHID)  branchname  from inv_t_directpurchase a,inv_t_directpurchaseitem b,inv_m_item c,  inv_m_division d,inv_m_vendorsinformation e,inv_m_itemgroup f, com_m_staff g ,   inv_m_vendorsgroup i, inv_m_model j, inv_m_tax k   WHERE a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO = b.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO    AND  b.CHR_ITEMID =c.CHR_ITEMID   AND b.CHR_TYPE ='I'     AND a.INT_DIVIID= d.INT_DIVIID    AND a.INT_VENDORID = e.INT_VENDORID    AND c.INT_ITEMGROUPID = f.INT_ITEMGROUPID     AND a.CHR_REF=g.CHR_EMPID    AND e.INT_GROUPID = i.INT_GROUPID   AND j.INT_MODELID = c.INT_MODELCODE   AND b.INT_TAXID = k.INT_TAXID   AND a.CHR_CANCEL = 'N'  AND a.DAT_ORDERDATE >= '2024-09-01'  AND a.DAT_ORDERDATE <= '2024-09-14'  ORDER BY a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO   )   UNION ALL    (SELECT a.CHR_VENDORPO VendorInvoice, DATE_FORMAT(a.DAT_ORDERDATE,'%d-%b-%Y') PODate, e.CHR_VENDORNAME NameoftheVendor,   e.CHR_GSTNO  VendorGSTNo,    j.CHR_MODELCODE Brand ,   b1.CHR_NAME ProductGroup, c.CHR_PRODUCTDESC PCODEDESCRIPTION  ,    FUN_INV_GET_WARRANTY_IN_YEAR(b.INT_WARRANTY) Warranty,  c.CHR_HSNCODE HSNorSAC ,  FUN_INV_GET_PURCHASE_SERIALNUMBER_CONCATS( b.CHR_TYPE,b.CHR_ITEMID,a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO) SlNos,   b.INT_QUANTITY Qty, b.DOU_UNITPRICE UnitCost, ROUND((b.INT_QUANTITY  * b.DOU_UNITPRICE),2) BeforeTax,  b.DOU_TAX_AMOUNT TaxAmt,  b.DOU_TOTAL Total, a.DOU_AMOUNT NetValue,     ROUND(a.DOU_TOTALAMOUNT,2)  FinalValue , k.CHR_TAXNAME GST,g.CHR_STAFFNAME AccountManager  , FUN_GET_BRANCH_NAME(a.INT_BRANCHID)  branchname  from inv_t_directpurchase a,inv_t_directpurchaseitem b,inv_m_produtlist c,  inv_m_productgroup b1, inv_m_division d,inv_m_vendorsinformation e,inv_m_productgroup f, com_m_staff g ,    inv_m_vendorsgroup i, inv_m_model j, inv_m_tax k   WHERE a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO = b.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO   AND b.CHR_ITEMID =c.CHR_PRODUCTID   AND b.CHR_TYPE ='P'     AND a.INT_DIVIID= d.INT_DIVIID    AND a.INT_VENDORID = e.INT_VENDORID    AND c.INT_PRODUCTGROUPID = f.INT_PRODUCTGROUPID    AND c.INT_PRODUCTGROUPID = b1.INT_PRODUCTGROUPID    AND a.CHR_REF=g.CHR_EMPID    AND e.INT_GROUPID = i.INT_GROUPID   AND j.INT_MODELID = c.CHR_MODEL   AND b.INT_TAXID = k.INT_TAXID   AND a.CHR_CANCEL =
